IIeW (The International Invitationals of e-Wrestling) — Card Archive

IIeW Night Two — 1 March 1998

1 March 1998
Content noticeA period document, served as recovered; withheld details are marked. It carries the humor, language, and content typical of its era and community — some of it crude, dated, or offensive, including material that wouldn't be written today. See Curation.
[The screen is white writing on blue lettering, as a warning appears on the

screen.] THE FOLLOWING IS PROPERTY OF THE INTERNATIONAL INVITATIONALS OF E-WRESTLING. UNAUTHORIZED DISTRIBUTION OF THIS BROADCAST WILL RESULT IN IMMEDIATE PROSECUTION TO THE FULLEST EXTENT OF THE LAW. AND A VISIT BY A BUNCH OF WRESTLERS WHO DIDN'T GET PAID... [The screen goes white as an instrumental version of "Highway To Hell" by AC/DC starts up. The gold-on-purple IIeW logo sits, shimmering as if carved

of jewels, in the middle of the screen as four small boxes appear in one

corner of the screen each. They flash the IIeW logo for a few seconds, and

then switch to clips.

Creed going for the Triangle Creedplex on Serge Annis, Jessica Starbird

hitting a spin kick on Napuleolekalani, Bishop in a dark alley, Serge Annis

prying Creed's eye out with barbed-wire, Casey James superplexing Requiem,

Curtis Hansen and Kevin Slater shaking hands, Mister Robinson raising his

hands in the air, J.W. Hardin getting pinned by Gabriel Whitecross, Steve

Kowalski grappling with Brody Thunder on top of a cage, Mad Dog Watkins

walking to the ring, "Playboy" Ronnie D defeating Dan Kauffman, and Brody

Thunder defeating Requiem.] ____________________________ / _____ _____ __ __ \ | |_ _|_ _| \ \ / / | | | | | | __\ \ /\ / / | | | | | | / _ \ \/ \/ / | | _| |_ _| || __/\ /\ / | | |_____|_____\___| \/ \/ | | INTERNATIONAL INVITATIONALS | | OF e-WRESTLING | \ ____________________________ / N * I * G * H * T T * W * O LIVE! From the SkyDome in Toronto, Ontario, Canada! March 1st, 1998 [Black fills the screen as "I Vow To Thee My Country," an old classical piece,

plays in the background.

Slowly, a gold-on-purple IIeW logo slowly fades in. A new shot fades in over the old one as a hulking trophy, with a one-foot tall

wooden base and two feet of solid silver on top making a long, narrow cup.

There are two silver handles per side. As a dry-ice smoke effect surronds the twirling cup, a voiceover begins.] Voiceover: Eight men. [In rapid succession, eight names shoot through the screen over the cup.] _ _ _ ___ ___ ___ _ _ | || | / \ | _ \ \_ _| \| | | __ |/ ~ \| / |) | || .` | |_||_/_/ \_\_|_\___/___|_|\_| ___ ___ _____ ___ _____ __ | _ \ __|_ _| _ \/ _ \ \ / / | _/ _| | | | / (_) \ \/\/ / |_| |___| |_| |_|_\\___/ \_/\_/ __ _____ ___ _ _ ___ _____ _____ _ _ \ \ / / __| _ \ || |/ _ \| __\ \ / / __| \| | \ V /| _|| / __ | (_) | _| \ V /| _|| .` | \_/ |___|_|_\_||_|\___/|___| \_/ |___|_|\_| _ ___ _____ ___ | \| \ \ / / __| __| | .` |\ V / (__| _| |_|\_| |_| \___|___| __ ____ _____ ___ ___ | \/ \ \ / / __| _ \/ __| | |\/| |\ V /| _|| /\__ \ |_| |_| |_| |___|_|_\|___/ _ ___ ___ ___ ___ | | | __| _ ) __/ __| | |__| _|| _ \ _| (__ |____|___|___/___\___| ___ ___ ___ ___ ___ / __| _ \ __| __| \ | (__| / _|| _|| |) | \___|_|_\___|___|___/ _ ___ ___ ___ | | / _ \ / __/ _ \ | |_| (_) | (_| (_) | |____\___/ \___\___/ Voiceover: One night. [A few quick clips engulf the screen. Creed hitting the GFA on a hapless

opponent. Joe Petrow swinging Maurice McArthur around in an airplane spin.

Chris Myers hitting the Rattler. LOCO pinning Rick Styles. Nik Nyce smashing

someone to the ground with the Nyceplex. Otto Verhoeven lifting an opponent up

for the Slaughterslam. And finally, J.W. Hardin hitting the Cattlebuster on

Bobby Taylor. Freeze on Simon Lebec winking into the camera with "Pappy Myers'" gold tooth in tow.] Voiceover: One goal. [The shot goes back to the cup.] Voiceover: The Deschenes Cup, by invitation only. [The camera pans upwards, and hovers over a shot of the top of the cup, and

slowly zooms closer to the bowl. The camera appears to enter inside the cup

and the shot quickly switches to an aerial view of the SkyDome, roof

retracted.

The lights glisten, as purple-and-yellow fireworks are set off around the

ring and around the roof. The camera switches to a long shot inside the

packed arena, looking at the entranceway. It zooms to a purple-and-yellow

desk, beside the entranceway, with the IIeW logo on the front.

Two men, Jeff Walters and Rod Riggins, sit at the desk. The former is dressed

in a purple blazer with a yellow tie. He is approximately 30 or so, and has

wavy brown hair, slicked back. Rod Riggins, on the other hand, has a yellow

blazer with a purple tie on, and wears purple-tinted round sunglasses. He

looks to be in his late-30's, and his blond hair is thinning.]

Walters: Ladies and gentlemen, welcome to Night Two of the International Invitationals of e-Wrestling! We are back, LIVE, at the SkyDome in picturesque -- Riggins: And smoggy! Walters: Toronto, Ontario, Canada! Welcome to all of our viewers who are

tuning in to the Double-Eye Ee Double-Ya for the first time. You sure missed

a SUPERB show last night, as, among other things, Jessica Starbird grappled

the IEWA's Women's TV Title away from Melody in amazing fashion, Gangsta

Impasse miraculously defeated "Cowboy" Ken Curtis for the SCRA World Title, Serge Annis brutally defeated Bishop in a Steel Cage duel with Bishop, "Crimson" Joe Reed put Danny Dynamite through a table to win the EMWC TV Title

and Casey James snuck his way to a victory over Gunnar Gaines and Requiem!

Riggins: And for those of you who had extra coin to spare, welcome back! Walters: Right... As you saw a few moments ago in our video promo, we are

staging the Deschenes Cup tonight, and we are almost ready to kick it off!

Riggins: But first, we have got to give the people the complete line-up for

tonight! Walters: Righteo! First off, we have the Deschenes Cup itself. [The screen switches to a purple background with yellow writing.] "Sychosys" Joe Petrow \ "Outlaw" J.W. Hardin / ~~~~~~~~~~~~~ \ Otto "The Butcher" Verhoeven \ / ~~~~~~~~~~~~~ \ "The Perfectionist" Nik Nyce / ~~~~~~~~~~~~~ | "The Savior" Simon Lebec \ | ~~~~~~~~~~~~~ "Diamondback" Chris Myers / ~~~~~~~~~~~~~ \ / Creed \ / ~~~~~~~~~~~~~ LOCO / ~~~~~~~~~~~~~ [The shot goes back to the anouncers.] Riggins: In the first match, Joe Petrow will take on "Outlaw" J.W. Hardin! I'm

gonna love this one!

Walters: Joe will have to watch out for The Syndicate, no doubt. Riggins: Joe's smart enough... After that, we will see the Teutonic Terror, Otto "The Butcher" Verhoeven take on "The Perfectionist" Nik Nyce. Walters: I don't really like either of these guys, but I have to say that Otto

is a much, MUCH, bigger man and will probably be able to pull this one off.

Riggins: That's who I give the nod to. Walters: And then, in the next bracket, "The Savior" Simon Lebec will take on

upstart Diamonback Chris Myers. Will Myers be able to take the wily veteran?

Riggins: I don't believe so... But, when you mix wrestling and a prize, you

never know what someone will come up with to win. Like mixing bowling and

beer...

Walters: Ha ha. Just because I enjoy the occasional game... Anyway, in the

last match of the first round, the Red-Gloved Warrior takes on LOCO!

Riggins: As sick as he makes me, I'd say Creed is the OVERWHELMING favorite. The only other man who could take this is J.W. Hardin. Now wouldn't THAT be a

final to remember!

Walters: That sends chills up my spine... Anyway, the EMWC will present Curtis "Thunder" Hansen versus "Wild Thing" Kevin Slater! These two have a bad grudge

going, and Slater sent Hansen into a... Manic-depressive state. I have to say,

Hansen is one of my favorites now. Riggins: Really? I don't care much for their music. Leave it to the teenie-boppers. Walters: CURTIS Hansen! Riggins: Is one of them named Curtis? Walters: Yuh-hunh... Then, we have the Deschenes Cup semi-finals! Riggins: Right after, the MLWO follows up last night's act with a Tag Team

title match as the Good Knights take on Rich and Infamous!

Walters: And then MLWO World Title vacator Captain Midnight takes on Daemon Dempsey! Riggins: Midnight'll cane Dempsey's ass out! Walters: Yeah... I guess... And then Mr. Robinson takes on THIRD DEGREE! Who

will THIRD DEGREE be? We'll find out tonight!

Riggins: I can't wait! But I absolutely CANNOT wait for the EMWC World Title

match as Gabriel Whitecross defends against my man, Tony Starks, who subs for

an injured Lord Byron!

Walters: Speaking of Byron, and the EMWC, Steve Kowalski takes on Byron's foe

from the EMWC, Mad Dog Watkins in a progressive death match for the IIWF World

Title! Riggins: And then... Then there will be two! In a best-two-out-of-three-falls

match, the Deschenes Cup will be awarded. I say it's going to Hardin, even

though Creed's the favorite.

Walters: I say Creed. Playing my cards safe... All this leading up to the main

event. In a steel cage, "Lone Wolf" Brody Thunder will butt heads with

hometown favorite "Playboy" Ronnie D! I say Ronnie takes this one!

Riggins: Well, I beg to differ! Brody all the way! When you're #1, you don't

lose to just anyone...

Walters: Oh! Oh! Ladies and gentlemen, IIeW ring announcer Michael Dufferin

is in the ring now! We're getting set for out first match!

________ _ __ ________________________________________________ / _/ _/__| | / / / DESCHENES CUP QUARTER-FINAL | / / / // _ \ | /| / / / "OUTLAW" J.W. HARDIN VS. "SYCHOSYS" JOE PETROW | _/ /_/ // __/ |/ |/ / / Written by: [name withheld] | /___/___/\___/|__/|__/ /____________________________________________________| [Suddenly, the theme of the 1998 Winter Olympics blasts over the speakers as IIeW ring announcer Michael Dufferin stands in the ring.] Dufferin: Ladies and gentlemen, boys and girls, WELCOME to Night Two of the International Invitationals of e-Wrestling, the GRANDEST SPECTACLE IN WRESTLING TODAY! [Big cheers erupt from the crowd!] Dufferin: The following match is a quarter-final match in the Deschenes Cup

tournament! Introducing first, he stands at six feet, three inches and weighs

two-hundred twenty-seven pounds. He hails from Tokyo, Japan, originally fro

the great state of Michigan, from the United States Olympic Curling team,

accompanied to the ring by "Mr. Majestyk" MAURICE MCARTHUR, "SYCHOSYS"

JOOOOOEEEEEEE PETROOOOOOOOOOOOWWWWWW! [The crowd cheers as Joe Petrow walks out in a red, white and blue workout

suit. Maurice follows behind him, ever his lackey. Joe reaches the ring, does

a few jumping jacks, and unzips his workout suit, revealing blue trunks with

blue kneepads and black boots. He hands the workout suit to McArthur and hops

into the ring.

Then, the arena lights go out and a lone red spotlight focuses on the

entranceway.] Dufferin: And, his opponent! He stands at six feet, ten inches and weighs

three-hundred fifty-four pounds. From Dry Gulch, Texas, accompanied to the

ring by Tony Starks, Casey James and Brian Lau, The Syndicate, "OUTLAW" J.W.

HAAAARDINNNN! [Hardin's dark appearance reflects his personality. He has dark eyes, black

shoulder-length hair (which he wears loose) and a black goatee. Once rather

bulky, he gained impressive muscle definition during a one-year stay in

Europe. On the way to the ring, he wears a black cowboy hat pulled low and a

duster. When he isn't wrestling, Hardin is rarely seen without his hat, still

pulled low so that it shadows his dark eyes.

He wears simple black tights and boots. A blood red "O" for "Outlaw" is

printed on each boot.

Hardin enters with his hat pulled low and walks slowly to the ring area,

followed by a smirking Casey James and Brian Lau, who also has a smile on his

face. Hardin ignores the fans along the way. Years of isolation have made him

indifferent to the fans of any federation. Once in the ring, Hardin grabs the

house microphone.

He points at Petrow and says...] Hardin: I know ya like them _preliminary_ rasslers, Petrow. Well I found

someone who thinks yer boy McArthur is just like you... overrated. Git on out

here!"

[IIWF jobber El Super Gecko appears at the top of the aisle, looking

resplendent in his colorful lizard luchador outfit and mask. He carries a

large paper bag and receives a mixed pop as he waddles down the aisle,

hissing at the IIeW fans who boo him.]

Walters: What the? El Super Gecko and Maurice McArthur? That'd be a match! [Starks, Lau and James exit the ring and join Gecko outside the ring. They

huddle for a moment and break up as the bell rings. Super Gecko still has his

paper bag...]

Walters: And we're ready to start Night Two! [Hardin looms over Petrow. His 6'10", 353-pound frame seems magnified two-fold

in comparison to Petrow's tiny 6'3", 227 lbs. He instantly grabs the scrawny

Petrow by the neck, choking him down to the ground. He quickly drops a knee

into Petrow's groin and steps back to admire his work.]

Riggins: Alright! Hardin taking it to that little punk, already! [Hardin rips Petrow off the mat and whips him to the ropes. Petrow goes flying

off the ropes and right towards Hardin. Hardin ducks for a backdrop. Petrow

freezes in his tracks, grabs Hardin for a DDT when suddenly, out of nowhere, a

kiwi hits him in the back of the head!]

Walters: What the?! [Brian Lau, Casey James and Tony Starks all crack up when they see Petrow

looking around. They pat El Super Gecko, the marksman, on the back in

congratulations of his shot.]

Riggins: Haha! It was Lizard-boy! [Petrow lets go, and furiously stomps the unripened fruit. This gives Hardin

enough time to kick Petrow in the rear end, sending him staggering into the

ropes.]

Riggins: What a shot by Hardin! [Suddenly, another kiwi flies into Petrow's face as El Super Gecko walks right

up to him and plants it in there. The crowd half-boos/half-laughs.]

Walters: What is going on? What IS up with this? Riggins: Can't a lizard-man have some fun? Walters: Not in serious competition! [Petrow wipes soggy kiwi fruit off his face and turns around. He is promptly

met with a huge European Uppercut, drawing "ooohs" and "aaahs" from the crowd.]

Riggins: I love this! [Hardin then waistlocks Petrow and El Super Gecko waddles for cover as Hardin

spins around and hoists himself and Petrow over the top rope! The crowd boos

and groans at the impact.]

Riggins: OUTLAW'S CURSE SUPLEX! Walters: Hardin ain't pulling any punches tonight! [Hardin tells Gecko to come over to the fallen Petrow. The rest of The Syndicate starts to converge around a semi-concious Petrow. Hardin tells Gecko

to throw a kiwi in Petrow's face...

As Gecko raises his hand and gets ready to launch a kiwi into Petrow's face, Mr. Majestyk runs behind Gecko and nails him with a double-axehandle. Hardin

promptly turns around and nails McArthur with a huge right. He then picks him

up and holds his arms behind his back. McArthur is barely concious enough to

struggle, and is hit with a kiwi that knocks him out -- cold. Gecko reaches in

his paper bag and throws another kiwi at Petrow. He then bends over and hisses

at him, "Majesssssssstyk Mauriccccccccce sssssssucksssssssss!"

Gecko quickly scampers away as Hardin throws Petrow back into the ring.] Walters: Oh... This is a MOCKERY of the sport... How can Dave Hopper, our

esteemed referee, permit this to go on?

[Petrow is hooked up for a DDT, not the Cattlebuster, however. Suddenly, his

hand juts out and Hardin is rolled up in a small package!]

Walters: Petrow with the 1... 2... Nope! [Hardin gets up quickly and slaps Petrow in the side of the head. Petrow

nearly falls down, and is whipped into the ropes.

As he hits the ropes, right near El Super Gecko, Gecko reaches out and cracks

him in the head with a lead pipe!]

Walters: Gecko hits Petrow with a pipe! The ref had his back turned! Riggins: I love it! [Hardin rushes over, picks Petrow up and quickly hits the Cattlebuster DDT to

a big round of boos!]

Riggins: CATTLEBUSTER DDT! Walters: Stick a fork in him, he's done! 1... 2... 3! Dufferin: Ladies and gentlemen, the winner of this bout and advancing to the

second round, "OUTLAW" J.W. HARDIN!

Walters: There's the official word. A farcical win for the Syndicate. ["Outlaw Blues" by Pat Benatar kicks up as Casey James, Brian Lau, Tony Starks

and the star of the match, El Super Gecko step into the ring. There is much

celebrating when suddenly, Casey James gets a mic from teh ring announcer.]

James: Wait! Wait! Cut the music! [The music abruptly stops.] James: Now, there's something in this picture that's missing. Is all of the Syndicate here? [James starts a head count...] James: There's Brian, there's Tony, there's J.W., there's me... Wait! Where's Claw? [Suddenly, El Super Gecko takes his mask off, and it turns out to be...] James: CLAW! We never had ANY idea! [With that, the Syndicate laughs their heads off as Tiger Claw pours the rest

of the kiwis on Petrow. "Outlaw Blues" by Pat Benatar kicks up once again as

the Syndicate leaves the ring to a chorus of boos, celebrating all the way.]

Walters: McArthur trying to revive the fallen Olympian, now. This is just

disgraceful... [Cut back to the booth.] Riggins: HILARIOUS! I loved that! Walters: How can you condone those actions? That was just despicable! Well,

there's no shame at all for Petrow, as he was out and out cheated. I can't

believe it... How can that happen at a prestigious event like the

International Invitationals of e-Wrestling? Riggins: With the Syndicate in the house... ANYTHING can happen! Walters: I see... Well, our next match in the Deschenes Cup tournament should

be a real scorcher. Sure, the fans won't care who wins as both men are

despised but it should really heat things up here in a hurry. The pure power

of Otto Verhoeven, "The Butcher" against the technical skills of "The

Perfectionist" Nik Nyce. The old game of power vs science is revisted. Rod, your pick in this one. Riggins: Well, this one's gonna be violent. Otto's been very determined in

the weeks before this night. He wants to make a big impact, a big

impression, and show the world that he's not done for, not by a long

shot. Nyce was a late entrant and may not be ready for what he's about

to face. My pick...Otto. In a bloodbath.

________ _ __ ________________________________________________ / _/ _/__| | / / / DESCHENES CUP QUARTER-FINAL | / / / // _ \ | /| / / / OTTO VERHOEVEN VS. NIK NYCE | _/ /_/ // __/ |/ |/ / / Written by: [name withheld] | /___/___/\___/|__/|__/ /____________________________________________________| JW: Well, we're about to find out. Let's go down to ring announcer Michael Dufferin for the ring announc-Here comes Heidi instead! RR: Va va VOOOOOOM!! Hey Heidi, I've got a fever...come take my

temperature baby!! JW: Would you stop? [Nurse Heidi walks out first, wearing a golden coloured, very tight-

fitting nurse costume. She strides to the ring with a few quick, but

confident steps. The men in the crowd cheer her on, a small pocket of

fans even starting a "HEI-DI"-chant. She enters the ring by stepping

through the ropes, pushes the announcer back and grabs his microphone.

With a bold whirl she stands in the middle of the ring, an arrogant

smile on her face.] Heidi: LAAAAADDDDIIIEEEEESSSSSSSS AND GEENNNNTTTLLLLEEMMMMEEEEEENNNNN! CHILLLLLLDDRREEEENNNNNN OF AAAAAAALLLLLLLLLLL AAAAAAGGGGEEEESSSSS!!! PRESENTING TO YOU ALL ... HAILING FROM ZE BEAUTIFUL CITY OF ESSEN IN ZE GREAT NATION OF GERMANY, STANDING SIX FEET AND EIGHT INCHES TALL AND VEIGHING ZREE HUNDRED AND FORTY POUNDS ... ZE SUPERIOR GERMAN AZLETE ... ZE TEUTONIC TERROR ... ZE GERMAN JUGGERNAUT ...ZE MAN WHO VILL SHOW ALL OF YOU TONIGHT HIS DEFINITION OF ZE TERM VIOLENCE ... HERE IS ZE BUTCHER ... OTTO VERHOEVEN! [The haunting tunes of "Halloween" begin to blare out of the PA and

suddenly the lights go out ... and strobe lights kick in. Slowly, the

massive form of Otto Verhoeven makes his way to the ring. He stops at

the head of the aisle, his facial expression serious, and raises both

fists in the air to the rising volume of the crowd boos. After a few,

long seconds he lowers his arms again and continues his way to the

ring, climbing up the steps and stepping over the top rope with ease.

Just then the strobe lights shut down and the regular lights are turned

back on and we get the first good look at Verhoeven. He has black hair

cut in a military crewcut. His blue eyes often glare with piercing

intensity. He wears a black wrestling singlet with crossing straps

similar to Ludvig Borga's, crimson fingerless leather gloves, a black

elbow pad on his right arm and black wrestling boots.. There is a red

and a golden stripe down his right leg. Heidi casts a smile at him

while he pulls at the ropes, eagerly awaiting his opponent.]

JW: Verhoeven appears to be in great shape. There was a lot of talk

before the tournament that that loss up in Portland was one that

crushed Otto, both physically and mentally. But he definitely appears

to be ready. Let's check out his opponent...

MD: And his opponent...weighing in at 239 pounds and hailing from Chicago, Illinois...he is..."THE PERFECTIONIST"...NIK NYYYYYYYYYYYYCE!!!!!! (Nyce walks out to Jimi Hendrix's version of the Star Spangled Banner

to a big heel pop. It's obvious that the crowd hates both of these

men. Nyce is dressed in a flowing silk robe, made to resemble...the

German flag. He carries in his hand a cordless mike. Stopping in the

aisle, he stares at Otto and Heidi and begins to speak.)

NN: Otto...you listening? Good. Well, you dumb ox. It's finally

here. The night when I'm gonna kick your goosestepping ass back to

Germany! You know, until I met you, I had a lot of respect for Germany. (Nyce begins taking off his robe, folding it so that the flag is facing

outwards.) NN: I mean, after the Wall came down, you guys weren't so bad. I did

a little checking on your country's history...and I think you all are a

bit misunderstood. Hitler had the right idea...

(The fans grow tired of Nyce's babbling and begins to boo him fiercely.) NN: Yep. He was right to try and exterminate an entire race, an

entire country. (Nyce takes out a Zippo lighter and begins igniting it.) NN: But he made a mistake! It wasn't the Jews he needed to get rid of. It

was people like you, and your worthless parents...and your whore fiancee!

(And then, with a flash, Nyce lights the robe, the German flag, ablaze! The crowd is stunned as Nyce begins to laugh, and even more so as Otto

charges down the aisle, and floors Nyce with a right hand.)

JW: Otto knocked him flat! What a sick human being Nik Nyce is! And Otto's making him pay for it now. "The Butcher" is a former boxer, and

the way he's pounding on Nyce, maybe he never should have quit.

RR: Otto's two sources of pride are Nurse Heidi and his homeland, and Nyce managed to insult them both. Verhoeven is dragging Nyce down

towards the ring, and he fires him into the ringpost! Nyce is probably

wishing he'd kept his mouth shut right now.

JW: Verhoeven rolls him into the ring, and we may be about to pay a

visit to the Slaughterhouse. Otto pulls him right back up...GORILLA

PRESS!!! SLAM TO THE MAT!!! What raw power from The Butcher! And he

pulls him right back up...again into the press-EYE RAKE BY NYCE! AND A

DDT! RR: Great counter by Nik Nyce, and he's on the attack now. Slaps on a

side headlock...BULLDOG!!! Excellent move, and Nyce is in control!

Putting the boots to Otto now, he's usually a pure science wrestler,

but will punish a man in any way he can.

JW: Nyce hit Otto with two high impact blows to the head, which has The Butcher dazed. Nyce drops a leg across the throat...and now he's

going up top. Nik Nyce, The Perfectionist, is up on the top

rope...FROG SPLASH...YES!!! One...two...kickout by Otto! Nurse Heidi

is shouting to Otto, giving him the encouragement he needs. Nyce stays

right on the attack though...and sits down on Otto with a Camel Clutch.

RR: This is one of Verhoeven's favorite moves, and is a great example

of Nyce trying to get into his opponent's head and change their game

plans. This is very likely to get Otto mad, which in some cases is a

bad idea, but other times can work to your advantage. Otto's trying to

power out of this hold...and he's succeeding. He's raising up off the

mat...what power!

JW: Otto Verhoeven is one of the strongest men in our sport, and he's

showing that power right now. Look at this...he's coming right off the

mat, and he's lifting Nyce up...DRIVES HIM INTO THE CORNER! AND AGAIN

TO THE CORNER! Otto spins around...punching bag time! Lefts...rights...great combos in there...and a big uppercut to polish

it off! And Nik Nyce is on dream street. Otto just pummeled him.

RR: And he's not done yet...hooks him...up...vertical suplex on the

way...HARD TO THE MAT! And now Otto's going to work on the back of

Nyce. Big elbowdrop on the back, and a second. All setting up that

brutal Slaughterslam!

JW: Verhoeven pulls up Nik Nyce...fires him to the corner...and

charges in with a lariat!! Thar rocked the ring! Fires him to the

other side...again with the big lariat! Nyce is out on his feet...OTTO

HOOKS HIM! POWERBOMB!!! Nyce must have bounced off the mat several

feet. And Otto making the cutthroat signal! I think we're about to

visit the Slaughterhouse...

RR: Otto hooks him by the throat...here he goes... JW: SLAUGHTERSLAM!!! CHOKESLAM RIGHT ON THE KNEE!!!! Nyce has

crumbled...Otto puts a fist in the chest. One...

TWO... THREEEEEEE!!!! MD: Your winner of this first round match..."THE BUTCHER"...OTTOOOOOO VERHOEVENNNNNNNNNNNN!!!!! JW: Big win for Otto here tonight, and he's gonna go on to face the

winner of the Joe Petrow-J.W. Hardin match. And whoever wins that one,

they've got a monster ahead of them in the form of this man. Dominant

win for Otto Verhoeven!

[Cut back to the booth. Riggins is jumping around. Why?] Riggins: I CALLED IT! I WAS RIGHT! OTTO WON! Walters: Don't have an orgasm... Geez... This isn't some kind of guessing

competition... Riggins: Oh, but it is! You stink at predictions and I want to show it to the

world! Walters: Yeah... Mhmmm... Well, up next, two men with a dislike for each

other, but not disrespect. Why? Well, recently, on the Ray Phalatio show...

Well, let's just roll it! (We open on a wildly applauding audience. Men and women of all races clap

wildly as the logo "The Raymond Phalatio Show" comes up on the screen. Coming

down the aisle between the audience is a young man holding a wireless mic,

wearing a blue suit. He has short brown hair, and is wearing a white shirt

underneath his sport coat and a red tie)

RP: Welcome to a special edition of the Raymond Phalatio show! (The crowd cheers wildly at this) RP: We have a great show, ladies and gentlemen. I am your host, Raymond Phalatio... (Once again, the crowd bursts into applause) RP: Thank you, thank you! Ok, we have a special show today, as this is a show

dedicated to professional wrestling!

(More cheers...you're starting to get the feeling there's a huge APPLAUSE sign somewhere...) RP: We have Hollywood Hogan, one of the biggest stars in the sport, talking

"THE SAVIOR".........SIMON LEEEEEEEBBBBBBBBBBEEEEEECCC!!!!! CM: WHAT??????????? (Lebec walks onto the stage, wearing his minister's garb. There

SL: He.......he was a teacher o' mine back down in th' South. An' we were havin' a grade school cook up wit all them fixins! He

some tin that ya don't even need.

(Lebec continues to look at the picture, intensity radiating from his eyes) An' ya got yer lil' wooden base, wit a nameplate attached. All in

(Lebec pauses, as he stares at the picture) Then, ya think o' the participants in this here tourney. Ya think o' Petrow..........voted one o' th' best by his peers. (Lebec pauses before he continues) An' then ya think o' Hardin.......an' Creed........an' Otto. All former

champeens in th' greatest fed in th' world.......th' Double-Eye.

(Lebec pauses once again.......a long pause) An' then.........then ya think o' yerself. What ya accomplished. An' ya see

hard.......pr'haps even harder.

(The crowd is hushed as Lebec continues) What ya fought s' long an' hard ta gain. What ya bled over........were

somethin' more precious than anythin' else on this here planet. It means

redemption..........an' a purpose ta go on.

(Lebec gives Myers one last look at the picture before putting it back in his

already done their provin'.

(Lebec pauses once again, clearing his throat before continuing) You an' me? Yeah, damn right we got somethin' ta prove. An' it sets us

out on a mission. Not a mission fer th' fans. Not a mission fer God. But a

mission fer ourselves. 'Cause we got some provin'. Maybe prove ta Long,

Spreadbury, Martin, an' the rest o' em that we got what it takes to be top

incorrect." But most o' all, maybe prove ta ourselves that th' hours we spend

doin' what we do each week hasn't gone unnoticed. Prove ta ourselves that

there's a reason fer being a Diamondback, a Savior, or even a Showstopper.

Yessir, you an' me are more alike than ya think, young Myers. But there's only one diff'rence that sep'rates me an' you. Ya wanna win that there piece

o' tin.......ta make a name fer yerself?

(Lebec pauses) I wanna win it. ta make th' name that I had. Pr' haps then them voices

will lemme sleep at night. Pr'haps then I can figure out where I left m'

head. Pr'haps then I'll have a reason ta keep playin' the game. Pr' haps

then I'll be able ta call m'self a man again.

(Lebec takes out another cigarette and lights it) So......I wish ya luck, young Myers. I really do. All the luck in th' world. Pr' haps even some o' th' luck I should be wishin' on m'self. 'Cause yer

gonna need it. I gotta lotta space on m' mantle, an' I 'tend on fillin' it

real soon. Startin' in March.

(Lebec extends his hand to Myers, who cautiously accepts it) I wish ya luck.......an' I'll pray fer ya. Pray that I don't hurt ya too bad. An' pray that ya make a name fer yerself. An' I'll pray that it won't be

made on March 1st. Rest easy, son. Yer gonna need it.

heavily, wincing all the while. Brody starts to stir a little.]

Riggins: Brody's alive! He's alive! Walters: Ronnie brings Brody to his feet, he whips him... [Brody flies into the cage and hits hard, sending the crowd into a frenzy! Brody holds his lower back in agony as he crumples to his knees.] Walters: What a shot! [With Brody on his hands and knees, Ronnie plants a boot in the lower back

area of Brody Thunder. He lifts him up by the bloody head, spins him around

and lifts him up...]

Walters: Brody picked up and... ATOMIC DROP! [Brody holds his groin and his back as he hops around while Ronnie clutches

his knee.] Walters: That may not have been the brightest move on Ronnie's part... [Ronnie gets up to his feet again and Brody turns around. Ronnie slams a right

fist into Brody's mouth, sending a spray of blood through the ring! Brody

staggers back and launches a fist into Ronnie's nose, sending MORE blood into

the ring!]

Walters: NOW we have a donnybrook! [Ronnie smashes a left into the side of Brody's head, sending blood out and

follows up instantly with a huge jab! Brody staggers back, kicks Ronnie in the

stomach and grabs his head. He puts Ronnie's head on the top of his and drops

down, smashing Ronnie's nose again! The crowd boos big-time.]

Walters: What a dirty move! Of all the low-down... Riggins: Calm down! It was beautiful! [Ronnie spins around on the ground, holding his bent nose.] Riggins: One good thing about what Brody's doing to Ronnie: After the match,

when Ronnie needs to get a nose job, they won't have to break his nose!

Walters: That's sick! Riggins: And funny! [Ronnie gets to his feet slowly, with Brody's assistance. Brody then puts Ronnie's head under his arm and sets him up for the Cattlebuster DDT! The

crowd is in shocked silence!]

Walters: Oh, no! Not this! [As Brody is about to fall back, Ronnie sends a huge fist into his groin area! Brody doesn't let go, but loosens his grip enough to allow Ronnie turn himself

around 180 degrees into position for a reverse DDT! Ronnie locks his waist and

pulls Brody up and over with a huge effort and holds Brody in position for a

Tombstone Piledriver.] Walters: He reversed it! Brody is in trouble now! [Ronnie falls down into a sitting position, crunching Brody's head into the

mat! The crowd goes wild!]

Walters: Huge piledriver! Huge piledriver! Riggins: Oh, no! [Ronnie lifts Brody to his feet and sets him up for a piledriver. As soon as Brody's head is between Ronnie's legs, he backdrops Ronnie high in the sky! The crowd boos as Brody bounces off the ropes and hits a running stomp on Ronnie's weak right knee! Brody walks back over to Ronnie D and he twists his

knee back for a half-Boston, holds it in place, gets up and drops his shin

against Ronnie's, causing a loud scream to come out of Ronnie.]

Riggins: Yeah! He's got him! [Brody stands again and then bends down to one knee. He then grabs Ronnie's

sore right knee and places it, knee-cap down, on his own knee. He then pushes

on the thigh and ankle of Ronnie D, effectively trying to snap Ronnie D's

knee!]

Walters: OH MY! Brody is trying to snap Ronnie's knee! [The crowd boos as Brody keeps applying pressure.] Riggins: Yeah! Kill him! [Brody continues to press, and now he starts pumping the leg.] Walters: Ronnie D in extreme pain now! Will he hang in there? [The colour from Ronnie's face is suddenly drained as Brody applies one last

huge thrust on Ronnie's knee. A huge scream comes from Ronnie, who was trying

to grit it out and a HUGE snap! The crowd explodes in boos as Thunder lets go,

and we can see that Ronnie's knee is severely bent.]

Walters: LORDY! I think they heard it in the upperdecks! Ronnie's knee just

snapped in two! Ligaments and tendons must have just been ripped apart!

[Brody stands up on his feet and slaps his hands together, signifying that

he's polished him off. He gazes up at the cage again, as the boos come down!]

Walters: Well, there is still hope, albeit slim. He DID get up after the Cattlebuster. Riggins: It took him a whole minute to get up, and besides, I think this may

have even been more devastating than that Cattlebuster!

Walters: I hate to say it, but you're probably right. [Brody swings a leg over the ropes, and then another. He then puts a foot on

the first bar. Ronnie, who has been writhing in agony all the while, sees

Thunder on the cage, five feet up, and bursts to his feet. He charges the cage

and vaults over the ropes. He starts climbing as fast as he can, as the crowd

explodes!]

Walters: OH MY GOODNESS! RONNIE D IS IN HOT PURSUIT OF BRODY! Riggins: COME ON, BRODY! Walters: This is a race to the finish! I still don't know HOW Ronnie is doing

this with that injury to his knee!

Riggins: The desire to win brings out the best, and the worst, in people. Let's see who's the best now, because it's brought the desire has brought it

out in both of them!

[Brody looks behind him and sees Ronnie, a huge grimace of pain on his bloody

face! He instantly speeds it up, as the camera pans to a wide shot. The crowd

cheers wildly as the two burst into a race for the top!

Brody quickly reaches ten feet, with Ronnie only five feet behind! Soon, Brody

reaches fifteen feet and soon Brody reaches the top!

He swings a leg over the top quickly, and by the time Ronnie reaches the top,

he has both over!

The crowd goes nuts, upping the decibel by a large margin as they are caught

up in the heat of the moment!]

Walters: This is it! It's Thunder or D now! One or the other! [Ronnie grabs Brody by the right hand to prevent him from climbing down. The

angers Thunder, who swings wildly at Ronnie! Brody's fist sails directly at

the side of Ronnie's head. What follows almost seems to go in slow motion...

The fist flies towards Ronnie's head, and without a fraction of a second to

spare, Ronnie ducks! As Brody's fist flies through a mess of brown-and-red

hair, the crowd goes nuts!]

Walters: OH MY! BRODY MISSES! [Ronnie quickly pushes Thunder's head down with his free right hand! He

underhooks the left arm of Thunder, and releases his grip on the right to

allow him to underhook it! He quickly hooks it and pulls hard to get Brody

vertical!]

Walters: RONNIEPLEX! IS RONNIE'S KNEE STRONG ENOUGH TO HANDLE THE WEIGHT?! [Suddenly, Ronnie loses his balance and falls back, Brody on top of him! As

if in slow motion, Ronnie thrusts his body to the left in mid-air! As he sails

towards the mat, his hair flying through the air majestically, his mouth wide

open in surprise, he completes the 180 degree turns without a second to spare

as Brody's face collides with the mat! They bounce off the mat with a loud

crash!]

Walters: _HE_HIT_IT_!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!! HEEEEEEEEE HIIIIIIIIIIT IIIIIIIIIT! Riggins: I... I... Uhhh... Ahhh... [Ronnie lies over an unconscious Brody Thunder as the house just goes nuts! Steamers of purple and gold fly through the crowd and into the ring, hitting

the two men slumped on the mat, and draping over the cage! Air horns blast

through the arena and are barely audible due to the noise!]

Walters: I... can't... BELIEVE IT! LISTEN TO THE CROWD! [After a good 15 seconds, Ronnie sits up. He slowly gets to his feet, and

clutches his knee in pain. He holds his knee as he limps to the cage. He rolls

under the ropes, so as not to damage his knee and uses the ropes to help

himself up!]

Riggins: Well... Well... Uhh... He still has to climb the cage! There's still

a chance! [Ronnie puts his left foot on the first bar. He then grimaces as he lifts his

damaged right knee up. As he tries to push himself up to get to the next bar,

he loses his footing and slips.

He lands on his feet at the bottom of the cage. He holds his knee for a few

seconds. Then, he takes a deep breath, holds it in, and puts his hands on the

bars. He puts his left foot up first and in a huge burst of energy, ascends

the cage!]

Walters: Ronnie was having trouble, but he's going at it full force now! He's

got it! [The crowd cheers wildly, even louder than before, as a large section chants "RON-NIE! RON-NIE!" Ronnie quickly reaches five feet, then ten. He pauses,

his face twisted in pain, takes a breath and digs in to go at it again. He

gets to fifteen, and then he reaches the top! The whole crowd is now chanting

"RON-NIE! RON-NIE!" as he swings his right leg over the top. As he sits on top of the cage, he raises his hand in the air eliciting a

louder chant! He swings his left leg over and turns around. He bounds down the

side of the cage, going down.]

Walters: LISTEN TO THE CROWD! [He reaches fifteen; ten; five!] Walters: DO YOU BELIEVE IN MIRACLES? [Ronnie leaps down the last five feet as the bell goes off and the crowd

erupts in cheers! Ronnie grimaces in pain as the ringside ref raises his hand!]

Walters: YES! YES! MIRACLES CAN HAPPEN! UNNNNNNBELIEVABLE! [IIeW ring announcer Michael Dufferin booms over the mike to the crowd's

delight!] Michael Dufferin: LADIES AND GENTLEMEN, THE WINNER... "PLLLLLAAAAAAYYYYYBOOOOY" RRRRRRRRRRRRRRRROOOOOOOOOOOOONNNNNNNNNNNNNNNIIIIIIIIIIEEEEEEEEEE DEEEEEEEEEEEE! ["Sharp Dressed Man" by ZZ Top blares over the sound system as purple and gold

balloons rain down on the crowd from the parts of the roof that aren't

retracted and the crowd goes wild!

Fireworks of green, gold, red, blue and all sorts of other colours explode

over the stadium sending the crowd into another frenzy!]

Walters: Incredible! Just incredible! [Ronnie falls down on his behind as he smiles with pride! The camera takes a

close-up of him resting against the guard-rail with his hand-raised. The

camera zooms in on his face, with dried blood covering his chin and his nose

decimated. His eyes are squinting, as he tries to capture the moment, and his

mouth stretched in the biggest, bloodiest smile you'll ever see.]

Walters: Ladies and gentlemen, I cannot believe what we've just witnessed! For Rod Riggins and the entire IIeW team, I'm Jeff Walters, the one who predicted

correctly, saying GOOD FREAKING NIGHT!

[The camera fades on Ronnie D, resting against the guard-rail, arm raised in

victory, balloons and streamers showering him.]

["Highway To Hell" by AC/DC starts up as slow motion clips start up... The SkyDome glistening in the night sky... Rod Riggins and Jeff Walters at the booth, talking... Joe Petrow and Maurice McArthur in the ring... "El Super Gecko" pulling away his mask to reveal that he is... Tiger Claw! Nik Nyce burning a German flag... Otto Verhoeven hitting the Slaughterslam... Simon Lebec pinning Chris Myers... Chris Myers raisinig his hands in the air... LOCO standing in the ring... Creed hitting the Creedplex... Curtis Hansen getting powerbombed by Kevin Slater... Hansen kicking Slater with the baseball spikes... Hansen and Slater shaking hands... Casey James spanking Heidi Uppenman... Verhoeven hitting a huge Military Press on Hardin... Hardin piledriving Verhoeven through a table... Creed going for the Goodnight, Farewell, Amen Powermbomb... Chris Myers hitting a huge Hurracanrana on Creed... Creed leaving through the crowd... IIeW President Ron Deschenes screaming at Creed... Jobba the Butt raising his hnds in the air... Captain Midnight pinning Daemon Dempsey... THIRD DEGREE ripping his mask away to reveal that he is Jerry Joblonski... Tony Starks pinning Gabriel Whitecross... IIeW President Ron Deschenes walking out... Gabriel Whitecross holding up the EMWC World Title... Steve Kowalski and Mad Dog Watkins standing in the ring, bloodied... Kowalski holding up the IIWF World Title... J.W. Hardin chokeslamming Chris Myers... Myers pinning Hardin with a small package... J.W. Hardin hitting the Cattlebuster DDT... Myers, slumped in the corner, unconcious... Hardin becomes a victim of the Rattler... Myers holding up the Deschenes Cup... The ring being set up for the last match... Brody Thunder making his way down the aisle... "Playboy" Ronnie D walking down the aisle... Brody snapping Ronnie's like a twig... Ronnie hitting the Ronnieplex... Ronnie celebrating against the railing... And finally, an aerial shot of the SkyDome... And the pictures and music fade.]
